planner_summary: Moe's Original BBQ was founded by Mike Fernandez, Ben Gilbert and Jeff Kennedy. After meeting at the University of Alabama, they instantly became friends and had a mutual interest in all things Southern: BBQ, blues, college football, and whiskey. In 1988, Mike learned to fire roast meats from Tuscaloosa legend Moses Day, whose distinct style makes up Moe's original flavor of BBQ. Eventually the founding partners all relocated to Vail, CO, where each refined their cooking skills. name: Moe's Original BBQ
is_active: true

entity_type: restaurant
entity_subtype: barbecue_restaurant
description: Moe's Original BBQ was founded by Mike Fernandez, Ben Gilbert and Jeff Kennedy. After meeting at the University of Alabama, they instantly became friends and had a mutual interest in all things Southern: BBQ, blues, college football, and whiskey. In 1988, Mike learned to fire roast meats from Tuscaloosa legend Moses Day, whose distinct style makes up Moe's original flavor of BBQ. Eventually the founding partners all relocated to Vail, CO, where each refined their cooking skills. Together, they created something special by using fruit wood to smoke their meats and complementing them with two sauces. A trip to Moe's Original BBQ means a chance to slow down, make connections and enjoy our quality recipes passed down for generations. When you dine at Moe's, you're always the guest. The Moe's Original team takes pride in ensuring that their customers enjoy themselves as much as their meal. Expect to be served top notch BBQ, soulful sides and a beer or bushwacker complemented by a smile and thank you. Today, Moe's Original BBQ has been named in the Top 10 BBQ Chains in America by USA Today and The Daily Meal, one of the fastest growing, emerging restaurant chains by Restaurant Business Magazine, and one of the Top 10 Rib Joints in the U.S. by Relish. The Orange Beach location, in the mid-2000s, was partnered with Mark "Pinto" White to test the BBQ waters of Alabama and it turned out to be a success. Since then, partners Lee Nelson and Matt Lovato joined Mark to keep the beach BBQ joint running smoothly. After an extensive fire in 2020, an Ole Hickory smoking pit was added along with more square footage to make the dining room, bathrooms and kitchen larger. There is now seating for 110, inside and outdoors. Additionally, salads, bushwackers and liquor drinks are available. Guests dining in now receive buzzers while waiting for their orders. Take out customers are texted when orders are ready and can grab them quickly from a designated pick up window. We also feature live music in the backyard when weather permits.

rating: 5
review_text: This place has the best BBQ and fixins on the entire coastal area! Really nice little place, the decor is so cute and the whole vibe of the place is wonderful. Friendly and courteous staff, great menu and prices are fair. They give nice size portions, too. I was there yesterday evening with my daughter and we got the sampler platter, which had a good assortment of meats and 3 sides. We chose the potato salad, slaw and baked beans. It came with 2 big pieces of cornbread and it was really good and flavorful, not dry and grainy at all. Their sweet tea is true southern sweet tea. We ate our fill and still took some back to the condo. Definitely recommend this place if you're traveling in Orange Beach and want some good BBQ. The aroma can be smelled as soon as you get out of your car. 5 stars on everything!

rating: 5
review_text: We found this awesome BBQ by smell. Sounds weird, but we could smell it long before we knew what it was and the smell sold us instantly.

We made the 1/2 mile trek down Perdido Beach Blvd and found it, the holy grail hidden amongst unsuspecting chain restaurants. From the stickers on the door to the awesome signs out front - we knew we were in for something good.

The nachos: amazing

The pulled pork: the most tender, fall apart pulled pork we have ever had. Anywhere (from Kansas City and beyond)

The smoked chicken: juicy and tender

The sides: I think one of the kids ate no less than five sides of baked beans. The Mac n cheese was a bit dry, but the humidity outside made up for it.

Seating (inside and outside) was ample and the outside area had the best play area for kiddos.

Will definitely be back!

id: 97b876c0-1793-4394-bf34-27769f0c7554
entity_slug: moes-original-bbq-orange-beach
reviewer_name: Elata Cummings
rating: 5
body: This place has the best BBQ and fixins on the entire coastal area! Really nice little place, the decor is so cute and the whole vibe of the place is wonderful. Friendly and courteous staff, great menu and prices are fair. They give nice size portions, too. I was there yesterday evening with my daughter and we got the sampler platter, which had a good assortment of meats and 3 sides. We chose the potato salad, slaw and baked beans. It came with 2 big pieces of cornbread and it was really good and flavorful, not dry and grainy at all. Their sweet tea is true southern sweet tea. We ate our fill and still took some back to the condo. Definitely recommend this place if you're traveling in Orange Beach and want some good BBQ. The aroma can be smelled as soon as you get out of your car. 5 stars on everything!

helpful_count: 0
approved: true

--- entity_reviews [row 2 of 5] ---
id: 91bae83e-c157-4808-b0d5-f9c8836e11d4
entity_slug: moes-original-bbq-orange-beach
reviewer_name: A. B.
rating: 5
body: We found this awesome BBQ by smell. Sounds weird, but we could smell it long before we knew what it was and the smell sold us instantly.

We made the 1/2 mile trek down Perdido Beach Blvd and found it, the holy grail hidden amongst unsuspecting chain restaurants. From the stickers on the door to the awesome signs out front - we knew we were in for something good.

The nachos: amazing

The pulled pork: the most tender, fall apart pulled pork we have ever had. Anywhere (from Kansas City and beyond)

The smoked chicken: juicy and tender

The sides: I think one of the kids ate no less than five sides of baked beans. The Mac n cheese was a bit dry, but the humidity outside made up for it.

Seating (inside and outside) was ample and the outside area had the best play area for kiddos.

Will definitely be back!

id: 122308b9-09df-4520-aacf-4bbe9f831fd1
entity_slug: moes-original-bbq-orange-beach
reviewer_name: BETH Dawson
rating: 5
body: My family and I loved Moe's! I love the laid-back atmosphere. There's seating available inside and outside. There's lots of cool art and pictures on the wall that I enjoyed looking at. The staff was friendly and helpful when ordering.
I had the loaded baked potato with smoked chicken, and it was delicious and very filling.
I tried the Ribs my husband ordered (I forgot to get a pic), but they were tender and flavorful.
The children enjoyed chicken tenders, grilled cheese, fried Catfish, mac & cheese and homemade chips! Despite being very picky they loved everything!
They offer different specials each day.
They have a nice stage out back with live music some nights. The location is convenient. Moe's was a hit with us, and I look forward to visiting again soon.

ref_type: business
ref_id: moes-original-bbq-orange-beach
entity_slug: moes-original-bbq-orange-beach
title: Moe's Original BBQ
category: barbecue_restaurant
subtitle: Moe's Original BBQ was founded by Mike Fernandez, Ben Gilbert and Jeff Kennedy. After meeting at the University of Alabama, they instantly became friends and had a mutual interest in all things Southern: BBQ, blues, college football, and whiskey. In 1988, Mike learned to fire roast meats from Tuscaloosa legend Moses Day, whose distinct style makes up Moe's original flavor of BBQ. Eventually the founding partners all relocated to Vail, CO, where each refined their cooking skills. Together, they created something special by using fruit wood to smoke their meats and complementing them with two sauces. A trip to Moe's Original BBQ means a chance to slow down, make connections and enjoy our quality recipes passed down for generations. When you dine at Moe's, you're always the guest. The Moe's Original team takes pride in ensuring that their customers enjoy themselves as much as their meal. Expect to be served top notch BBQ, soulful sides and a beer or bushwacker complemented by a smile and thank you. Today, Moe's Original BBQ has been named in the Top 10 BBQ Chains in America by USA Today and The Daily Meal, one of the fastest growing, emerging restaurant chains by Restaurant Business Magazine, and one of the Top 10 Rib Joints in the U.S. by Relish. The Orange Beach location, in the mid-2000s, was partnered with Mark "Pinto" White to test the BBQ waters of Alabama and it turned out to be a success. Since then, partners Lee Nelson and Matt Lovato joined Mark to keep the beach BBQ joint running smoothly. After an extensive fire in 2020, an Ole Hickory smoking pit was added along with more square footage to make the dining room, bathrooms and kitchen larger. There is now seating for 110, inside and outdoors. Additionally, salads, bushwackers and liquor drinks are available. Guests dining in now receive buzzers while waiting for their orders. Take out customers are texted when orders are ready and can grab them quickly from a designated pick up window. We also feature live music in the backyard when weather permits.
